Title: Материалы для истории раскола за первое время его существования, под редакцией Н. Субботина; том пятый: сочинения первых расколоучителей, часть вторая: сочинения бывшего юрьевецкого протопопа Аввакума Петрова (vol. V: the Works of the former Archpriest of Yurievets, Avvakum Petrov)
Creator: Avvakum Petrov (1620/21-1682); edited by Nikolai Ivanovich Subbotin (1827-1905)
Publisher: Moscow: Bratstvo sv. Petra mitropolita
Publication Date: 1879

Content note: Avvakum's writings other than the Life: his petitions to the Tsar from Pustozersk, his epistles to his followers and to Boyarynia Morozova, his dogmatic letters and his commentaries, with Subbotin's introduction; the census's Pustozersk letters and petitions in the standard nineteenth-century edition.
